Verify notes

  • Built by E6-S1. Copy password (rail, field button, entry menu, ⌘C) copies in Rust; the value never reaches the screen layer. Known difference, card over mockup: APP-03.21 "Password copied. Clears in 10 s." for the mockup's "Password copied · clipboard clears in 30 s" (10 s is Larsal's clear delay); the box is narrower for the shorter words (about 245 against 301 pt, centred on the same x); its place (centred, 20 above the foot), height 40, radius 10, --primary fill, ink, glyph and shadow are as drawn. The notice goes after 2.6 s, as in the mockup.
